declare void @willrt_init()
declare void @willrt_shutdown()
define i32 @main() {
entry:
  call void @willrt_init()
  call void @"std/errors.will$init"()
  %t563 = call ptr @"example.com/concurrency/async.will$main.task"(i32 -2, i32 0)
  %t565 = alloca i32
  %t564 = call i32 @willrt_join(ptr %t563, ptr %t565, ptr null)
  %t566 = icmp ne i32 %t564, 0
  br i1 %t566, label %main.async.fail.58, label %main.async.ok.58
main.async.fail.58:
  call void @willrt_shutdown()
  ret i32 1
main.async.ok.58:
  %t567 = load i32, ptr %t565
  call void @willrt_shutdown()
  ret i32 %t567
}

  store ptr null, ptr %t609
  br label %task.error.msg.done.59
task.error.msg.done.59:
  ret ptr %t599
}
